# Values-check of the step-15b RESULTS entry and Code's ping to the seat (results/raw/2026-10-15_m-code-to-design_step15b.md),
# before their commit: two adversarial passes (each three lenses -- the entry's facts, the ping's facts, the two side by side
# with the committed raws and messages -- each lens with a strict refuter) and a targeted third check of the last revision,
# with Code's disposition of every finding. Code stamp: 2026-10-15 05:55 UTC (date -u). Model-free; no agent opened data/m/ckpt/*,
# data/m/reports/* or the G-F0m-Qwen stage raw.
# Versions: the filled drafts (entry15b.filled.md, ping15b.filled.md) -> v2 (pass 1's fixes) -> v3 (pass 2's) -> the final
# texts (the targeted check's, if any). The diffs between versions are below.

====================================================================================================
PASS 1
====================================================================================================

---- THE WORKFLOW SCRIPT, verbatim
export const meta = {
  name: 'step15b-valuescheck-pass1',
  description: 'Values-check pass 1 of the step-15b entry and ping: facts of each, and side-by-side consistency, each with a refuter',
  phases: [{ title: 'Check' }, { title: 'Refute' }],
}
const S2 = '/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step15b'
const R = '/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab'
const PI = `THE PI'S WORDS (chat, the authoritative text; quotations of them must match these exactly, curly quotes as here):
(1) "Seat’s step-15 answers: results/raw/2026-10-14_m-design-to-code_step15_answers.md, sha256 07cd8e5f4535c95c61428a07c426490e694d16601818096d48210c534f780767. Commit by path. PI 1: patch v8 of the root copy is coming from the seat today; hold the table until I say “placed.” PI 2: PREPRINT-v1_1-draft.md is at root, untracked, as the reference. PI 3: commit the decisions block. PI 4: proceed in the answers’ order; v1.0’s commit waits on my cap."
(2) "PI 1: the preprint of record is placed — PREPRINT-v1_0-draft.md at root, patch v8, sha256 45b67246f2b2bcb40774e95462ad4e3c5f559fdd0358387cceec0155bebe7a41 (patch v5’s five items on the audited v7 copy; its header note lists the gated sentences). Build the table from it, inside the analyzer round the seat’s answers order; PIN-17 re-verified against it there."`
const COMMON = `
CONTEXT. You values-check Code's RESULTS entry and Code's ping to the design seat for step 15's second analyzer round
(PROPOSAL-m, repo ${R}). The texts under check (drafts, in scratch):
- the entry: ${S2}/entry15b.filled.md (it will be inserted at the top of RESULTS.md's "## Log — Phase 2"; @@STAMPS@@,
  @@PINGSHA@@ and @@VC@@ are still placeholders, by design);
- the ping: ${S2}/ping15b.filled.md (it will be committed as results/raw/2026-10-15_m-code-to-design_step15b.md).
Sources of record (read what each claim needs; cite file:line): git (git log, git show) in ${R} — commits 5dca58b, 1523cce,
ed8e886; the analyzer at HEAD scripts/m_analyze.py; data/m/gates/G-ANL-F0.json (the new manifest, uncommitted) and
data/m/gates/G-ANL-F0.superseded-2026-10-15.json (the old); data/m/gates/m_posting_sentences.placeholder.json;
results/raw/2026-10-14_m-design-to-code_step15_answers.md (the seat's answers); results/raw/2026-10-15_m-analyzer-round2-step15_review.txt;
results/raw/2026-10-15_m-table-draft-step15.txt; results/raw/2026-10-15_m-ganl-f0-rerun-step15b.txt;
results/raw/2026-10-15_m-analyzer-round2-step15_sitecheck.txt; RESULTS.md (the step-15 entry below, from e4f0da3); LOCK-m-F0.md;
PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md; PREPRINT-v1_0-draft.md (v8) and PREPRINT-v1_1-draft.md; results/raw/2026-10-10_m-design-to-code_pre-LOCK-v0.1_round2.md (R2).
Scratch working files you may also read: ${S2}/table/ (spec.md, draft_rows_v1.json, draft_report_v2.txt), ${S2}/final_r7_summary.txt,
${S2}/final_r7_distinct.json, ${S2}/disp_r1.md, ${S2}/disp_r3.md, ${S2}/disp_final.md.
${PI}
HARD RULES. Read nothing under data/m/ckpt/ or data/m/reports/; never open results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gf0m-qwen.txt; never run git
inside jlens-qwen36/; modify nothing anywhere except files in your own work dir under ${S2}/vc1/; do not read scripts/audit_note_*,
results/raw/*note-*, README-NOTE.md, RESEARCH-NOTE-*, AUDIT-note-*, LICENS*, root *.svg. Never compute or quote any stage duration,
and no size or time metadata of data/m/ckpt or data/m/reports.
WHAT TO CHECK. Every factual claim: hashes (full and abbreviated: an abbreviation is the first 8 and last 4 hex), commit ids,
counts (checks, ids, rows, runs, mutants and their classes), line citations (:N), quotations (verbatim against their source,
the PI's against the text above), dates, what changed and what did not, attributions (what is the seat's, the PI's, Code's),
and any claim of order ("before", "after", "first"). Also: any inclusive claim ("every", "all", "none", "no ... survives")
must be backed by an inventory you run. Report each defect with the exact text, the evidence, and a replacement text.`
const SCHEMA = { type: 'object', properties: {
  findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, doc: { type: 'string', enum: ['entry', 'ping', 'both'] }, text: { type: 'string' },
    severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['error', 'imprecise', 'note'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, suggested_text: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'doc', 'text', 'severity', 'evidence', 'suggested_text'] } },
  verified: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['findings', 'verified'] }
const VER = { type: 'object', properties: {
  verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { id: { type: 'string' },
    verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PARTLY'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, suggested_text: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'suggested_text'] } },
  missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['verdicts', 'missed'] }
const LENSES = [
  { key: 'entry', task: 'Check the ENTRY claim by claim against the sources (the ping only where the entry refers to it).' },
  { key: 'ping', task: 'Check the PING claim by claim against the sources, including every quotation of the seat, every figure about the table draft (rows, boundary count, the rows firing in all 1,080 tuples and their list), the v8 line citations, and Q4\'s list of Code\'s additions against the analyzer\'s actual texts.' },
  { key: 'side-by-side', task: 'Read the entry, the ping, the two committed raws\' headers, and the commit messages of 1523cce and ed8e886 SIDE BY SIDE. Find every statement that two of them make differently (a count, a hash, a name, an attribution, an order, what is next), every claim one makes that another contradicts, and anything the entry states that the ping should and does not (or the reverse).' },
]
const res = await pipeline(LENSES,
  l => agent(COMMON + `\n\nTASK (${l.key}): ${l.task} Work dir ${S2}/vc1/${l.key}.`, { label: 'vc1:' + l.key, phase: 'Check', schema: SCHEMA }),
  (rep, l) => rep ? agent(COMMON + `\n\nYOU ARE A STRICT REFUTER of this values-check report (${l.key}), verbatim:\n${JSON.stringify(rep)}\nFor EACH finding reproduce it against the sources and give CONFIRMED / REFUTED / PARTLY with your evidence and the best replacement text. Default to REFUTED if you cannot reproduce. Add to 'missed' anything wrong in the texts that the report did not find. Work dir ${S2}/vc1/ref_${l.key}.`, { label: 'vc1-ref:' + l.key, phase: 'Refute', schema: VER }).then(v => ({ lens: l.key, report: rep, refute: v })) : null)
return res

export const meta = {
  name: 'step15b-valuescheck-pass2',
  description: 'Values-check pass 2 of the step-15b entry and ping: facts of each, and side-by-side consistency, each with a refuter',
  phases: [{ title: 'Check' }, { title: 'Refute' }],
}
const S2 = '/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step15b'
const R = '/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab'
const PI = `THE PI'S WORDS (chat, the authoritative text; quotations of them must match these exactly, curly quotes as here):
(1) "Seat’s step-15 answers: results/raw/2026-10-14_m-design-to-code_step15_answers.md, sha256 07cd8e5f4535c95c61428a07c426490e694d16601818096d48210c534f780767. Commit by path. PI 1: patch v8 of the root copy is coming from the seat today; hold the table until I say “placed.” PI 2: PREPRINT-v1_1-draft.md is at root, untracked, as the reference. PI 3: commit the decisions block. PI 4: proceed in the answers’ order; v1.0’s commit waits on my cap."
(2) "PI 1: the preprint of record is placed — PREPRINT-v1_0-draft.md at root, patch v8, sha256 45b67246f2b2bcb40774e95462ad4e3c5f559fdd0358387cceec0155bebe7a41 (patch v5’s five items on the audited v7 copy; its header note lists the gated sentences). Build the table from it, inside the analyzer round the seat’s answers order; PIN-17 re-verified against it there."`
const COMMON = `
CONTEXT. You values-check Code's RESULTS entry and Code's ping to the design seat for step 15's second analyzer round
(PROPOSAL-m, repo ${R}). The texts under check (drafts, in scratch):
- the entry: ${S2}/entry15b.v2.md (it will be inserted at the top of RESULTS.md's "## Log — Phase 2"; @@STAMPS@@,
  @@PINGSHA@@ and @@VC@@ are still placeholders, by design);
- the ping: ${S2}/ping15b.v2.md (it will be committed as results/raw/2026-10-15_m-code-to-design_step15b.md).
Sources of record (read what each claim needs; cite file:line): git (git log, git show) in ${R} — commits 5dca58b, 1523cce,
ed8e886; the analyzer at HEAD scripts/m_analyze.py; data/m/gates/G-ANL-F0.json (the new manifest, uncommitted) and
data/m/gates/G-ANL-F0.superseded-2026-10-15.json (the old); data/m/gates/m_posting_sentences.placeholder.json;
results/raw/2026-10-14_m-design-to-code_step15_answers.md (the seat's answers); results/raw/2026-10-15_m-analyzer-round2-step15_review.txt;
results/raw/2026-10-15_m-table-draft-step15.txt; results/raw/2026-10-15_m-ganl-f0-rerun-step15b.txt;
results/raw/2026-10-15_m-analyzer-round2-step15_sitecheck.txt; RESULTS.md (the step-15 entry below, from e4f0da3); LOCK-m-F0.md;
PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md; PREPRINT-v1_0-draft.md (v8) and PREPRINT-v1_1-draft.md; results/raw/2026-10-10_m-design-to-code_pre-LOCK-v0.1_round2.md (R2).
Scratch working files you may also read: ${S2}/table/ (spec.md, draft_rows_v1.json, draft_report_v2.txt), ${S2}/final_r7_summary.txt,
${S2}/final_r7_distinct.json, ${S2}/disp_r1.md, ${S2}/disp_r3.md, ${S2}/disp_final.md.
${PI}
HARD RULES. Read nothing under data/m/ckpt/ or data/m/reports/; never open results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gf0m-qwen.txt; never run git
inside jlens-qwen36/; modify nothing anywhere except files in your own work dir under ${S2}/vc2/; do not read scripts/audit_note_*,
results/raw/*note-*, README-NOTE.md, RESEARCH-NOTE-*, AUDIT-note-*, LICENS*, root *.svg. Never compute or quote any stage duration,
and no size or time metadata of data/m/ckpt or data/m/reports.
PASS 2. These texts are Code's revision after values-check pass 1, whose findings and refuter verdicts are verbatim in ${S2}/vc1.json. Check (i) that every CONFIRMED or PARTLY finding of pass 1 is fixed in the revision as its evidence requires (or that Code's different fix is also right), and (ii) that the revision introduces no new error. Then, as pass 1 did:
WHAT TO CHECK. Every factual claim: hashes (full and abbreviated: an abbreviation is the first 8 and last 4 hex), commit ids,
counts (checks, ids, rows, runs, mutants and their classes), line citations (:N), quotations (verbatim against their source,
the PI's against the text above), dates, what changed and what did not, attributions (what is the seat's, the PI's, Code's),
and any claim of order ("before", "after", "first"). Also: any inclusive claim ("every", "all", "none", "no ... survives")
must be backed by an inventory you run. Report each defect with the exact text, the evidence, and a replacement text.`
const SCHEMA = { type: 'object', properties: {
  findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, doc: { type: 'string', enum: ['entry', 'ping', 'both'] }, text: { type: 'string' },
    severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['error', 'imprecise', 'note'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, suggested_text: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'doc', 'text', 'severity', 'evidence', 'suggested_text'] } },
  verified: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['findings', 'verified'] }
const VER = { type: 'object', properties: {
  verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { id: { type: 'string' },
    verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PARTLY'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, suggested_text: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'suggested_text'] } },
  missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['verdicts', 'missed'] }
const LENSES = [
  { key: 'entry', task: 'Check the ENTRY claim by claim against the sources (the ping only where the entry refers to it).' },
  { key: 'ping', task: 'Check the PING claim by claim against the sources, including every quotation of the seat, every figure about the table draft (rows, boundary count, the rows firing in all 1,080 tuples and their list), the v8 line citations, and Q4\'s list of Code\'s additions against the analyzer\'s actual texts.' },
  { key: 'side-by-side', task: 'Read the entry, the ping, the two committed raws\' headers, and the commit messages of 1523cce and ed8e886 SIDE BY SIDE. Find every statement that two of them make differently (a count, a hash, a name, an attribution, an order, what is next), every claim one makes that another contradicts, and anything the entry states that the ping should and does not (or the reverse).' },
]
const res = await pipeline(LENSES,
  l => agent(COMMON + `\n\nTASK (${l.key}): ${l.task} Work dir ${S2}/vc2/${l.key}.`, { label: 'vc2:' + l.key, phase: 'Check', schema: SCHEMA }),
  (rep, l) => rep ? agent(COMMON + `\n\nYOU ARE A STRICT REFUTER of this values-check report (${l.key}), verbatim:\n${JSON.stringify(rep)}\nFor EACH finding reproduce it against the sources and give CONFIRMED / REFUTED / PARTLY with your evidence and the best replacement text. Default to REFUTED if you cannot reproduce. Add to 'missed' anything wrong in the texts that the report did not find. Work dir ${S2}/vc2/ref_${l.key}.`, { label: 'vc2-ref:' + l.key, phase: 'Refute', schema: VER }).then(v => ({ lens: l.key, report: rep, refute: v })) : null)
return res

export const meta = {
  name: 'step15b-valuescheck-pass3',
  description: 'Targeted third values-check of the v2->v3 changes to the step-15b entry and ping, with a refuter',
  phases: [{ title: 'Check' }, { title: 'Refute' }],
}
const S2 = '/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step15b'
const R = '/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab'
const COMMON = `
CONTEXT. A targeted third values-check of Code's RESULTS entry and ping for step 15's second analyzer round (repo ${R}).
Texts: ${S2}/entry15b.v3.md and ${S2}/ping15b.v3.md. The changes under check are exactly the hunks of ${S2}/entry_v2_v3.diff
and ${S2}/ping_v2_v3.diff, made on values-check pass 2's findings (verbatim, with refuter verdicts, in ${S2}/vc2.json; pass 1's in
${S2}/vc1.json). Sources: git in ${R} (commits 5dca58b, 1523cce, ed8e886; scripts/m_analyze.py and scripts/m_sessions.py at HEAD);
results/raw/2026-10-15_m-analyzer-round2-step15_review.txt, results/raw/2026-10-15_m-table-draft-step15.txt,
results/raw/2026-10-15_m-analyzer-round2-step15_sitecheck.txt, results/raw/2026-10-15_m-ganl-f0-rerun-step15b.txt,
results/raw/2026-10-14_m-design-to-code_step15_answers.md, LOCK-m-F0.md, PREPRINT-v1_0-draft.md; scratch ${S2}/final_r7_summary.txt,
${S2}/final_r7_distinct.json, ${S2}/equivalents_all.json, ${S2}/table/draft_table_v1.json and draft_table_v2.json, ${S2}/final_reviews.json.
HARD RULES. Read nothing under data/m/ckpt/ or data/m/reports/; never open results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gf0m-qwen.txt; no git in
jlens-qwen36/; modify nothing except files under ${S2}/vc3/; do not read scripts/audit_note_*, results/raw/*note-*, README-NOTE.md,
RESEARCH-NOTE-*, AUDIT-note-*, LICENS*, root *.svg. No stage duration, no size or time metadata of data/m/ckpt or data/m/reports.`
const SCHEMA = { type: 'object', properties: {
  findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, doc: { type: 'string', enum: ['entry', 'ping', 'both'] }, text: { type: 'string' },
    severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['error', 'imprecise', 'note'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, suggested_text: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'doc', 'text', 'severity', 'evidence', 'suggested_text'] } },
  verified: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['findings', 'verified'] }
const VER = { type: 'object', properties: {
  verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: { id: { type: 'string' },
    verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PARTLY'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, suggested_text: { type: 'string' } },
    required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'suggested_text'] } },
  missed: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } }, required: ['verdicts', 'missed'] }
phase('Check')
const chk = await agent(COMMON + `\n\nTASK: For EVERY added line in the two diffs, verify each factual claim against the sources (counts by your own
inventory, hashes, line citations, quotations verbatim, attributions, order). Also confirm each pass-2 CONFIRMED or PARTLY finding is
resolved by its hunk, and that no hunk contradicts any unchanged statement elsewhere in the entry or the ping. Work dir ${S2}/vc3/check.`,
  { label: 'vc3:check', phase: 'Check', schema: SCHEMA })
phase('Refute')
const ref = await agent(COMMON + `\n\nYOU ARE A STRICT REFUTER of this check report, verbatim:\n${JSON.stringify(chk)}\nFor EACH finding reproduce it and give
CONFIRMED / REFUTED / PARTLY with your evidence and the best replacement text; default to REFUTED if you cannot reproduce. Re-verify 3 of its
'verified' items. Add to 'missed' anything wrong in the changed hunks that it did not find. Work dir ${S2}/vc3/refute.`,
  { label: 'vc3:refute', phase: 'Refute', schema: VER })
return { check: chk, refute: ref }

---- CODE'S DISPOSITIONS OF PASS 3 (disp_vc3.md), verbatim
# Code's dispositions of the targeted third check (one checker, one strict refuter; 9 findings, 2 refuter misses)

All nine findings are applied in the final texts (entry15b.final.md, ping15b.final.md), in the refuter's form where the
verdict was PARTLY (VC3-5, VC3-8).

- **VC3-1** (error): v3's "No mutant that changes what the analyzer computes survives …" overclaimed. The text gaps edit
  the register that ganl writes into its manifest, and "compute the same on every input" is stronger than the
  equivalents' recorded reasons. It now reads, as pass 2's refuter had it: "No non-equivalent mutant of the analyzer's
  logic survives". The equivalents are equivalent, each with its reason; the accepted gaps edit texts or the battery,
  and a register text among them changes only what the manifest's `pins` records. The entry's correction of the
  committed "analyzer behaviour" is read the same way.
- **VC3-2** (error): the critics' challenges are in the review raw's critic sections; the assembly log holds Code's
  reasons.
- **VC3-3:** the PIN index's "Code's reading" excepts what R2 2 made the seat's within PIN-9 and PIN-10 (lock §9.3).
- **VC3-4:** "the class checks' unedited fixtures" for "every unedited fixture".
- **VC3-5:** "Code corrected the spec's unit citations, as the review raw's SET ASIDE records", without "only".
- **VC3-6, VC3-7:** the critics' notes: 238.1's consequence if read as a heading assertion; 244.3, 244.5 and 288.3 if
  the seat takes 61.3; the review raw's lines; "the spec's rule (a) (Q1's "Nulls")".
- **VC3-8:** the commit message's §9.3 citation corrected in the refuter's form.
- **VC3-9:** re-wrapping and quote form.
- **The refuter's misses:** the ping's pointer names all four sources the entry's corrections list corrects (the
  commit message, the review raw's header, the table raw, disp_final); FIN-FID-9's edit also narrowed s7.1-title's
  description to the main thesis, the adjudicator's reading, and both texts now say so.
